HEARTHLANDS: AGE OF TILLAGE — FULL RULES TUTORIAL
3000 cards total · rendered with players: Mike, Dana, Rosa
Each card is one phone screen. Tier = absurdity level (0 = sounds real, 4 = fully absurd).


======================================================================
GETTING STARTED: HEARTHLANDS
======================================================================

--- Card 1 · WELCOME · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
WELCOME TO THE HEARTHLANDS
Welcome, Mike, Dana and Rosa. Hearthlands: Age of Tillage is a game of farming, building, and trading for 1–5 players, played right here on this phone.
Before the first turn, this tutorial will teach you how to play.
[icons: reeve, cottage, barley]

--- Card 2 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
ONE PHONE, MANY TILLERS
Hearthlands is pass-and-play. On your turn, you hold the phone. When your turn ends, hand it to the player on your left.
Mike goes first, since Mike is already holding it.
[icons: pawn_red, pawn_blue, pawn_cream, pawn_orange, pawn_green]

--- Card 3 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE GOAL
Each player leads a farming Household. Build Cottages, Granaries, and Mills, connect them with Cart Roads, and trade wisely.
The first Household to earn 10 Respect wins.
[icons: respect, laurel, cottage]

--- Card 4 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
HOW THIS TUTORIAL WORKS
Part One introduces every piece you'll see on the board. Part Two walks through a single Turn, one Step at a time.
Tap Next to continue. The game begins as soon as the Turn has been explained.
[icons: rulebook, scroll]


======================================================================
PART ONE · THE PIECES: THE BOARD
======================================================================

--- Card 5 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE BOARD
The app deals a fresh board of hexagonal tiles at the start of every game. Each tile is a type of terrain, and most terrain produces one resource.
Pinch to zoom. Drag to look around.
[icons: barley_rise, peat_bog, mutton_downs]

--- Card 6 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
BARLEY RISE
Gentle golden hills. Barley Rise produces Barley.
It is the most common tile, and the most peaceful.
[icons: barley_rise, barley]

--- Card 7 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
FLAX FIELDS
Rows of pale blue flowers. Flax Fields produce Flax.
[icons: flax_fields, flax]

--- Card 8 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
MUTTON DOWNS
Open pasture, dotted with sheep. Mutton Downs produce Mutton.
The sheep are counted every Turn. See the Livestock Stage.
[icons: mutton_downs, mutton]

--- Card 9 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
PEAT BOG
Damp, dark, and useful. The Peat Bog produces Peat.
The center tile of every board is a Peat Bog.
[icons: peat_bog, peat]

--- Card 10 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
WICKER MARSH
Willows and reeds. Wicker Marsh produces Wicker.
[icons: wicker_marsh, wicker]

--- Card 11 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
LOAMFIELDS
Freshly plowed furrows. Loamfields produce Loam, which improves the yield of neighboring tiles.
[icons: loamfields, loam]

--- Card 12 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
BRINE FLATS
Shallow salt pools. Brine Flats produce Brine, which is used for rations and for preserving Mutton.
[icons: brine_flats, brine]

--- Card 13 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
TALLOW MINES
A rocky hillside with a timber entrance. Tallow Mines produce Tallow.
Nobody is entirely sure why Tallow is mined.
[icons: tallow_mines, tallow]

--- Card 14 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE UNMAPPED HEX
Every board contains one blank tile. It produces nothing.
You can tap it. We'd recommend that you don't.
[icons: the_unmapped]


======================================================================
PART ONE · THE PIECES: RESOURCES
======================================================================

--- Card 15 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
RESOURCE CARDS
Resources are held as cards in your hand, shown along the bottom of your screen when you hold the phone.
Keep your hand hidden from other players by tilting the phone away from them.
[icons: card_back]

--- Card 16 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
BARLEY
Used for Cottages, Granaries, and Almanac cards. Also the main ingredient in several Steps of the Turn.
[icons: barley]

--- Card 17 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
FLAX
Used for Granaries and Mills. Flax can also be woven, though the app does not currently support weaving.
[icons: flax]

--- Card 18 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
MUTTON
Used for Cottages and for feeding Drovers. Mutton spoils unless salted with Brine.
[icons: mutton]

--- Card 19 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
PEAT
Used for Cart Roads and Mills. Peat keeps Cottages warm during Frostmonth.
[icons: peat]

--- Card 20 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
WICKER
Used for Cart Roads, Cottages, and Wattle Fences. Lightweight and dependable.
[icons: wicker]

--- Card 21 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
LOAM
Loam is not spent. It is spread. Each Loam spread on a tile raises that tile's yield for the rest of the game.
[icons: loam]

--- Card 22 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
TALLOW
Used for candles, which extend your working hours into the Quiet Hour.
Tallow melts in warm weather. See the Tallow Melt Check.
[icons: tallow]

--- Card 23 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
BRINE
Used for rations and for salting Mutton. Each Drover requires Brine every Turn.
[icons: brine]

--- Card 24 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
TURNIPS
Turnips are not produced by any tile. They simply appear, usually when you have no room for them.
[icons: turnip]

--- Card 25 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
GOSSIP
Gossip is a resource that is passed between players by whispering the name of the card.
It cannot be spent, only spread.
[icons: gossip]

--- Card 26 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
RESPECT
Respect is your score. It is shown as bronze medallions next to your Household name.
The first player to 10 Respect wins.
[icons: respect]


======================================================================
PART ONE · THE PIECES: BUILDINGS
======================================================================

--- Card 27 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
COTTAGE
Your first building. A Cottage collects resources from each tile it touches. Worth 1 Respect.
To build, drag a Cottage from your supply onto a corner of the board.
[icons: cottage]

--- Card 28 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
GRANARY
An upgraded Cottage. Collects twice as many resources and stores surplus. Worth 2 Respect.
[icons: granary]

--- Card 29 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
CART ROAD
Roads connect your buildings. You may only build new buildings at the end of your own Cart Roads.
Drag along the edge of a tile to place one.
[icons: cart_road]

--- Card 30 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
MILL
A Mill converts two of one resource into one of another, once per Turn. Worth 1 Respect.
[icons: mill]

--- Card 31 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
WELL
A Well supplies Brine-free water to nearby Drovers, reducing their ration needs.
[icons: well]

--- Card 32 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
DROVER
A Drover moves your livestock and Hay Carts along Cart Roads. Drovers need Mutton and Brine every Turn, and good morale.
[icons: drover]

--- Card 33 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
TITHE BARN
A Tithe Barn protects nearby buildings from the Tax Assessor, but pays a tithe of its own to the Reeve.
[icons: tithe_barn]

--- Card 34 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
WATTLE FENCE
A Wattle Fence marks your boundary. Fences block Drovers, Hay Carts, and most sheep.
[icons: fence]

--- Card 35 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
HAY CART
Hay Carts carry resources between your buildings. Each cart has an axle that wears down with use.
[icons: cart]

--- Card 36 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
SCARECROW
A Scarecrow protects one tile from Blight. It also protects against the Goose, in theory.
[icons: scarecrow]

--- Card 37 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
HEDGEROW
Hedgerows grow on their own along boundaries. They can be trimmed, but they remember.
[icons: hedge]

--- Card 38 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
CHAPEL OF MILD CONCERN
A small chapel that grants 1 Respect to its owner.
It is concerned about something. It has not said what.
[icons: chapel]


======================================================================
PART ONE · THE PIECES: CHARACTERS
======================================================================

--- Card 39 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
CHARACTERS
Characters are neutral pieces controlled by the app. They move on their own during the Turn.
You can tap a character to see what it's planning. It won't always tell you.
[icons: reeve, assessor, goose]

--- Card 40 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE REEVE
The village official. The Reeve blocks production on whichever tile he stands on, and settles disputes.
[icons: reeve]

--- Card 41 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE TAX ASSESSOR
Walks the Cart Roads collecting the Levy. His route is calculated every Turn. His lunch break is not negotiable.
[icons: assessor]

--- Card 42 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE TINKER
Wanders the board repairing Hay Cart axles, for a price.
[icons: tinker]

--- Card 43 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
GERALD, THE COMMUNAL OX
Gerald belongs to everyone. Any player may borrow Gerald to pull a Hay Cart, if Gerald isn't tired.
[icons: ox]

--- Card 44 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE GOOSE
The Goose moves after every Stage. It cannot be removed from the board.
It is best not to have opinions about the Goose.
[icons: goose]

--- Card 45 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE UNWELCOME BARD
The Bard improves Drover morale by singing. The Bard lowers everyone else's morale by singing.
[icons: bard]

--- Card 46 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE VILLAGE IDIOT
Moves randomly. Votes in all disputes. His vote counts double.
[icons: idiot]

--- Card 47 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE MAGISTRATE OF HEDGES
Rules on boundary disputes. Always brings shears. Always uses them.
[icons: magistrate]

--- Card 48 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE HEDGE WITCH
Sells remedies for Blight and Plague. Payment is in Flax, or in something she'll name later.
[icons: witch]

--- Card 49 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE LOST PILGRIM
Always holding the map upside down. The Pilgrim is trying to reach the Unmapped Hex.
[icons: pilgrim]

--- Card 50 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE WEATHER PROPHET
Predicts the weather one Turn in advance. He is right about half the time, which he considers excellent.
[icons: prophet]

--- Card 51 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE RAT KING
Lives beneath the most crowded Granary. Holds court once per Turn. You will be expected to attend.
[icons: rat_king]


======================================================================
PART ONE · THE PIECES: TOKENS
======================================================================

--- Card 52 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
TOKENS
Tokens track everything else. They appear on your Household screen and on the board. Tap any token to see what it does.
[icons: harvest_die, grudge, favor]

--- Card 53 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE HARVEST DIE
The app rolls the Harvest Die for you at the start of each Turn. Every tile showing the rolled number produces.
The Harvest Die has thirteen sides.
[icons: harvest_die]

--- Card 54 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
WEATHER CUBE
Shows Sun, Rain, or Fog. The weather changes how tiles produce, how fast Tallow melts, and how the Goose feels.
[icons: weather_cube]

--- Card 55 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
ALMANAC CARDS
Buy Almanac cards to gain one-time advantages. Each card is worded carefully. Read them carefully.
[icons: almanac]

--- Card 56 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
FAVOR OF THE REEVE
Earned by following the rules exactly. A Favor lets you ignore one Step of your choosing.
[icons: favor]

--- Card 57 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
LEDGER PAGES
The Ledger records every trade, tax, and dispute. You can review the Ledger at any time by swiping down.
[icons: ledger]

--- Card 58 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
REEVE'S SEAL
Makes a trade or ruling official. Unsealed trades may be reversed during the Levy Stage.
[icons: wax_seal]

--- Card 59 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
GRUDGE TOKENS
Any player may declare a Grudge against another player, a tile, or a character. Grudges affect trading, voting, and scoring.
[icons: grudge]

--- Card 60 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
DEBT PEBBLES
Taken when you can't pay the Levy. Debt Pebbles grow each Turn. Slowly, then not slowly.
[icons: debt]

--- Card 61 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
BLIGHT CUBES
Blight spreads from tile to tile, reducing yield. Scarecrows and the Hedge Witch can help.
[icons: blight]

--- Card 62 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
RUMOR CHITS
Rumors are placed face-down on other players' Households. Nobody may check what a Rumor says until the Reckoning Stage.
[icons: rumor]

--- Card 63 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
PLAGUE MARKERS
If Plague reaches a Cottage, that Cottage stops producing until the Quarantine Phase is resolved.
[icons: plague]

--- Card 64 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE DAWDLING HOURGLASS
Some Steps are timed. The Dawdling Hourglass runs during those Steps.
It runs slower when you look at it.
[icons: hourglass]


======================================================================
PART ONE · THE PIECES: HOUSEHOLDS
======================================================================

--- Card 65 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
HOUSEHOLD COLORS
Each player's pieces appear in their Household color: red, blue, cream, orange, or green. Mike is red.
[icons: pawn_red, pawn_blue, pawn_cream, pawn_orange, pawn_green]

--- Card 66 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE BEIGE HOUSEHOLD
Beige belongs to the Tax Assessor. No player may choose beige.
If the app ever shows you in beige, see the Levy Stage immediately.
[icons: pawn_beige, assessor]

--- Card 67 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE COMPASS
The board always faces north. North is indicated by the compass in the corner of the screen.
North may change during the Turn.
[icons: compass]

--- Card 68 · NOTE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THAT'S EVERY PIECE
You've now met every piece in Hearthlands, Mike.
Next, Part Two explains a single Turn. A Turn has a few Stages.
[icons: rulebook, cottage, goose]


======================================================================
PART TWO · SEQUENCE OF PLAY: TURN 1
======================================================================

--- Card 69 · WELCOME · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
PART TWO: SEQUENCE OF PLAY
Hearthlands is played in Turns. Each Turn is divided into Stages. Each Stage is divided into Phases, each Phase into Segments, and each Segment into Steps.
Players perform every Step, in order. Most Steps only take a moment. Turn 1 begins now.
[icons: scroll, hourglass, harvest_die]


======================================================================
TURN 1 · STAGE I: THE DAWN STAGE
======================================================================

--- Card 70 · Stage I · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
THE DAWN STAGE
Players determine who acts first, what the weather will be, and what the land produces.
This Stage has 4 Phases: Initiative Determination, Weather Determination, Harvest Die Roll, and Production.
[icons: harvest_die, respect, weather_cube]


======================================================================
TURN 1 · STAGE I · PHASE A: INITIATIVE DETERMINATION
======================================================================

--- Card 71 · Stage I · Phase A · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE DETERMINATION
This Phase is resolved Segment by Segment. Results from earlier Stages may be needed; they can be found in the Ledger.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 72 · Step I.A.1.1 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RECORDING INITIATIVE
Each player calculates their Assessment Base: add the number of Drovers they control, subtract the number of Ledger pages they hold, then halve the result (round toward the Reeve).
[icons: drover, ledger]

--- Card 73 · Step I.A.1.2 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE TIE-BREAK
The app highlights every hex containing Peat. Starting with Rosa, each player selects one highlighted hex and records its yield on a Ledger page.
[icons: peat]

--- Card 74 · Step I.A.1.2 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE TIE-BREAK
This Step is performed once per Hay Cart, not once per player. With five players, this may take a moment.
[icons: cart]

--- Card 75 · FAQ · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
FREQUENTLY ASKED QUESTION
Q: Can I trade with players who aren't taking their turn?
A: No. You may only trade with the active player.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 76 · TABLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE RATING TABLE
Each Harvest Die: +1 Initiative Rating
Each Drover: +4 Initiative Rating
Each Weather Cube: +4 Initiative Rating
Each Respect point: +4 Initiative Rating
[icons: harvest_die, drover, weather_cube, respect]

--- Card 77 · Step I.A.1.3 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RESOLVING DISCREPANCIES
Each player secretly selects a number from 1 to 6 on their Household screen. Reveal simultaneously. The highest number adds it to their Convoy Capacity; everyone else subtracts 1.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 78 · Step I.A.1.3 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RESOLVING DISCREPANCIES
Should the supply of Peat run out during this Step, resolve the shortfall in player order, beginning with the player who most recently built a Drover.
[icons: peat, drover]

--- Card 79 · NOTE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
DESIGNER'S NOTE
Cart Roads are cheap on purpose. Early expansion keeps the game moving.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 80 · Step I.A.2.1 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RECORDING INITIATIVE
The app rolls the Harvest Die. On a 4 or higher, proceed to the next Step. Otherwise, the active player must place a Harvest Die on the Flax Fields, then repeat this Step.
[icons: harvest_die, flax_fields]

--- Card 81 · Step I.A.2.1 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RECORDING INITIATIVE
Results are recorded in the Ledger before any player may act on them. Unrecorded results may be challenged until the end of the Weather Determination Phase.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 82 · NOTE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
DESIGNER'S NOTE
Some players ask why Tallow is worth more than Barley. Have you ever tried to make a candle out of Barley?
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 83 · Step I.A.2.2 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SIMULTANEOUS RESOLUTION
For each Brine Flats tile adjacent to at least two Hay Carts, the app deducts 1 Mutton from the owner with the lowest Spoilage Value. Ties are broken by Step I.C.6.6.
[icons: brine_flats, cart, mutton]

--- Card 84 · Step I.A.2.2 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SIMULTANEOUS RESOLUTION
Modifiers from Step I.D.1.2 apply, except during High Harvest, when the modifiers from Step I.B.1.1 apply instead.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 85 · Step I.A.2.3 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RECORDING INITIATIVE
Starting with the player to the right of the active player and proceeding counter-clockwise, each player may either pass or reduce their Ration Deficit by 1 at a cost of 2 Tallow.
[icons: tallow]

--- Card 86 · Step I.A.2.3 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RECORDING INITIATIVE
Players may not trade while this Step is being resolved, except to settle obligations created by Step I.B.3.4.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 87 · Step I.A.2.4 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE TIE-BREAK
Pass the phone to Mike. They adjust each player's Assessment Base by the modifiers listed in Step I.C.3.2, then pass the phone back.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 88 · Step I.A.3.1 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE ROLL
Any Well that was built this Turn is considered unsettled. Unsettled buildings contribute half their normal Supply Level, rounded down.
[icons: well]

--- Card 89 · ERRATA · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXCEPTION TO STEP I.A.5.1
That Step does not apply if a Tithe Barn is within two hexes. In that case, Rosa must instead discard half their Resource cards, rounded down.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 90 · Step I.A.3.2 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE TIE-BREAK
If the Weather Cube shows Rain, add 1 to every player's Spoilage Value. If it shows Fog, add 1 only to players who cannot see the Tallow Mines. If it shows Sun, proceed to Step I.D.5.9.
[icons: tallow_mines]

--- Card 91 · Step I.A.3.2 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE TIE-BREAK
Negative results are recorded as Debt Pebbles. Debt Pebbles are not Barley and may not be used to pay for this Step.
[icons: barley]

--- Card 92 · Step I.A.3.2: Example · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE TIE-BREAK
Dana controls 2 Wells, and their Assessment Base is 6. Following Step I.A.6.7, Rosa must discard one Tallow.
Nobody scores this round.
[icons: well, tallow]

--- Card 93 · Step I.A.3.3 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RECORDING THE RESULT
The active player taps each Hay Cart they control, in clockwise order. For each one next to the Tallow Mines, add 1 to their Yield Modifier.
[icons: cart, tallow_mines]

--- Card 94 · Step I.A.3.4 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE TIE-BREAK
The active player may spend 1 Barley to reduce their Yield Modifier by 7. This may be done no more than twice per Segment, and never during the Initiative Determination Phase.
[icons: barley]

--- Card 95 · Step I.A.3.4 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE TIE-BREAK
This Step is performed once per Hay Cart, not once per player. With five players, this may take a moment.
[icons: cart]

--- Card 96 · Step I.A.4.1 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
ROUNDING
The active player may spend 1 Barley to reduce their Initiative Rating by 3. This may be done no more than twice per Segment, and never during the Weather Determination Phase.
[icons: barley]

--- Card 97 · Step I.A.4.1 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
ROUNDING
Negative results are recorded as Debt Pebbles. Debt Pebbles are not Brine and may not be used to pay for this Step.
[icons: brine]

--- Card 98 · Step I.A.4.1: Example · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
ROUNDING
Mike controls 2 Granaries, and their Initiative Rating is 3. Following Step I.B.1.1, Mike must reroll the Harvest Die.
Mike asks if this is a real Step. It is.
[icons: granary]

--- Card 99 · Step I.A.4.2 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE MODIFIER FOR ABSENT PLAYERS
The active player may spend 1 Loam to reduce their Ration Deficit by 2. This may be done no more than twice per Segment, and never during the Weather Determination Phase.
[icons: loam]

--- Card 100 · Step I.A.4.2 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE MODIFIER FOR ABSENT PLAYERS
Players may not trade while this Step is being resolved, except to settle obligations created by Step I.C.5.8.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 101 · NOTE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
DESIGNER'S NOTE
We playtested this Step for eleven years. We think it's close.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 102 · FAQ · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
FREQUENTLY ASKED QUESTION
Q: Does a Hay Cart count as a building?
A: For scoring, yes. For the Levy, no. For the Almanac, it depends.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 103 · Step I.A.4.3 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE RE-ROLL ELIGIBILITY
The active player may spend 1 Tallow to reduce their Ration Deficit by 2. This may be done no more than twice per Segment, and never during the Initiative Determination Phase.
[icons: tallow]

--- Card 104 · Step I.A.5.1 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RESOLVING DISCREPANCIES
The app rolls the Harvest Die. On a 5 or higher, proceed to the next Step. Otherwise, the active player must discard one Brine for each Hay Cart they control, then repeat this Step.
[icons: brine, cart]

--- Card 105 · Step I.A.5.1 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RESOLVING DISCREPANCIES
Negative results are recorded as Debt Pebbles. Debt Pebbles are not Loam and may not be used to pay for this Step.
[icons: loam]

--- Card 106 · Step I.A.5.2 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE MODIFIER FOR ABSENT PLAYERS
Each Granary with a Cart Road connection to the Loamfields may transfer up to 5 Tallow to another Well its owner controls. Transfers in excess of 5 spoil.
[icons: granary, loamfields, tallow, well]

--- Card 107 · NOTE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
DESIGNER'S NOTE
We playtested this Step for eleven years. We think it's close.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 108 · Step I.A.5.3 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE ROLL
Each Tithe Barn with a Cart Road connection to the Barley Rise may transfer up to 6 Brine to another Drover its owner controls. Transfers in excess of 6 spoil.
[icons: tithe_barn, barley_rise, brine, drover]

--- Card 109 · Step I.A.5.3 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE ROLL
This Step is performed once per Wattle Fence, not once per player. With five players, this may take a moment.
[icons: fence]

--- Card 110 · Step I.A.5.4 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
ORDER OF RESOLUTION
Compare each player's Barley to their Mutton. The difference is that player's Yield Modifier for the rest of this Phase. It will be needed in Step I.C.1.2.
[icons: barley, mutton]

--- Card 111 · Step I.A.5.4 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
ORDER OF RESOLUTION
The Initiative Rating calculated here is used three more times this Turn. Players are advised to remember it.
[icons: harvest_die, respect]

--- Card 112 · Step I.A.6.1 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
INITIATIVE TIE-BREAK
If the Weather Cube shows Rain, add 1 to every player's Supply Level. If it shows Fog, add 1 only to players who cannot see the Tallow Mines. If it shows Sun, proceed to Step I.A.2.7.
[icons: tallow_mines]

--- Card 113 · Step I.A.6.2 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
VERIFICATION
Each Hay Cart with a Cart Road connection to the Loamfields may transfer up to 7 Loam to another Mill its owner controls. Transfers in excess of 7 spoil.
[icons: cart, loamfields, loam, mill]

--- Card 114 · TABLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---

HARVEST DIE ROLL
A player with no Drovers performs this Step as if they had one, then removes it.
[icons: drover]

--- Card 216 · Step I.C.5.4 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
YIELD ROUNDING
Each player secretly selects a number from 1 to 5 on their Household screen. Reveal simultaneously. The highest number adds it to their Assessment Base; everyone else subtracts 1.
[icons: harvest_die, barley_rise, flax_fields]

--- Card 217 · Step I.C.5.5 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
HARVEST DIE ROLL
Starting with the player to the right of the active player and proceeding counter-clockwise, each player may either pass or reduce their Yield Modifier by 1 at a cost of 2 Tallow.
[icons: tallow]

--- Card 218 · Step I.C.6.1 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
YIELD ROUNDING
Compare each player's Brine to their Loam. The difference is that player's Initiative Rating for the rest of this Phase. It will be needed in Step I.A.1.2.
[icons: brine, loam]

--- Card 219 · Step I.C.6.1 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
YIELD ROUNDING
Players may not trade while this Step is being resolved, except to settle obligations created by Step I.C.6.5.

A Granary replaces the Cottage it upgrades. The Cottage returns to your supply.

ROUNDING
Compare each player's Barley to their Brine. The difference is that player's Yield Modifier for the rest of this Phase. It will be needed in Step I.D.1.4.
[icons: barley, brine]

--- Card 223 · Step I.C.6.2 (cont.) · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
ROUNDING
Negative results are recorded as Debt Pebbles. Debt Pebbles are not Barley and may not be used to pay for this Step.

--- Card 224 · Step I.C.6.2: Example · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
ROUNDING

[icons: cart, loam]

--- Card 225 · Step I.C.6.3 · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
ROUNDING

[icons: brine]

[icons: mill, granary, brine_flats, barley]

TURN 1 · STAGE IV · PHASE E: SPOILAGE
======================================================================

--- Card 882 · Stage IV · Phase E · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE
The active player holds the phone for this Phase. Other players may watch but may not tap.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 883 · Step IV.E.1.1(f) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILED GOODS DISPOSAL
The Goose inspects each Tithe Barn in turn. For each one, the app rolls the Harvest Die: on a 13, Mike must remove a Granary from the board and apologize to it; on any other result, recalculate Axle Wear.
[icons: goose, tithe_barn, granary]

--- Card 884 · Step IV.E.1.2(f) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILED GOODS DISPOSAL
The player holding the most Turnip becomes the Warden of Gossip for this Segment. The Warden must honk once before anyone else may continue.
[icons: turnip, gossip]

--- Card 885 · Step IV.E.1.2(f) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILED GOODS DISPOSAL
If the Goose moved during this Step, undo the Step, then undo the undoing.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 886 · Step IV.E.1.3(f) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SALTING WITH BRINE
Add together every player's Yield Modifier. If the total is odd, Gerald, the Communal Ox moves one hex toward the Loamfields. If it is even, the Goose moves anyway.
[icons: ox, loamfields, goose]

--- Card 887 · Step IV.E.1.3(f) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SALTING WITH BRINE
Results are recorded in the Ledger before any player may act on them. Unrecorded results may be challenged until the end of the Counter-Offer Phase.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 888 · Step IV.E.1.3(f): Example · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SALTING WITH BRINE
Mike controls 7 Scarecrows, and their Hedge Pressure is 3. Following Step III.D.6.9, Rosa must discard one Turnip for each Well they control.
Everyone agrees this is fair.
[icons: scarecrow, turnip, well]

--- Card 889 · Step IV.E.1.4(b) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE ROLL
Any player may declare an objection to Step I.D.6.7. Objections are resolved by comparing Stew Points; the loser must narrate, in character, what their Hedgerow had for breakfast.
[icons: hedge]

--- Card 890 · Step IV.E.1.5(e) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE ROLL
Any player may declare an objection to Step IV.C.2.3. Objections are resolved by comparing Grudge Weight; the loser must take a Weather Cube.
[icons: weather_cube]

--- Card 891 · Step IV.E.2.1(f) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE ROLL
The Unwelcome Bard inspects each Drover in turn. For each one, the app rolls the Harvest Die: on a 13, Mike must declare a Grudge against Dana; on any other result, recalculate Supply Level.
[icons: bard, drover]

--- Card 892 · Step IV.E.2.2(e) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE GRIEVANCES
Add together every player's Convoy Capacity. If the total is odd, the Tinker moves one hex toward the Flax Fields. If it is even, the Unwelcome Bard moves anyway.
[icons: tinker, flax_fields, bard]

--- Card 893 · Step IV.E.2.2(e) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE GRIEVANCES
Any player who sighed during this Step adds 1 Grudge token to their Household.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 894 · Step IV.E.2.3(d) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
MODIFIER STACKING
Each Scarecrow with a Cart Road connection to the Peat Bog may transfer up to 4 Tallow to another Granary its owner controls. Transfers in excess of 4 spoil.
[icons: scarecrow, peat_bog, tallow, granary]

--- Card 895 · Step IV.E.2.4(d) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE GRIEVANCES
Any player may declare an objection to Step I.C.3.5. Objections are resolved by comparing Axle Wear; the loser must hide a Blight cube on their Household screen.
Record the result in the Ledger.
[icons: blight]

--- Card 896 · Step IV.E.2.4(d) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE GRIEVANCES
Any Debt Pebble gained during this Step is held in escrow until Step I.D.3.3, then released, then taken back.
[icons: debt]

--- Card 897 · NOTE · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
DESIGNER'S NOTE
The Harvest Die has thirteen sides. Our lead programmer insists this is fine. Our lead programmer has thirteen fingers.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 898 · Step IV.E.2.5(b) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SIMULTANEOUS RESOLUTION
Each Scarecrow with a Cart Road connection to the Peat Bog may transfer up to 7 Mutton to another Tithe Barn its owner controls. Transfers in excess of 7 spoil.
The Reeve's Seal is required before continuing.
[icons: scarecrow, peat_bog, mutton, tithe_barn]

--- Card 899 · CLARIFICATION · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
CLARIFICATION
Turnip and Flax are different resources, though they taste similar.
[icons: turnip, flax]

--- Card 900 · TABLE · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
AXLE WEAR TABLE
Every Mutton traded to Mike: +4 Axle Wear
Each Scarecrow: +5 Axle Wear
Every Gossip traded to Dana: +3 Axle Wear
Control of the Loamfields: +5 Axle Wear
[icons: mutton, scarecrow, gossip, loamfields]

--- Card 901 · FAQ · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
FREQUENTLY ASKED QUESTION
Q: Is Gossip a resource or a condition?
A: Both, depending on the season.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 902 · Step IV.E.3.1(a) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILED GOODS DISPOSAL
Recalculate Axle Wear using the results from Step II.B.4.9, not the results from Step III.B.2.4, unless the Goose is asleep (see Goose Awakening).
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 903 · ERRATA · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ERRATA: STEP III.A.1.1
Replace every instance of "Mutton" with "Turnip". Where this creates a contradiction, see Step III.A.4.6.
[icons: mutton, turnip]

--- Card 904 · Step IV.E.3.2(a) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE GRIEVANCES
Add together every player's Grudge Weight. If the total is odd, the Village Idiot moves one hex toward the Peat Bog. If it is even, the Village Idiot moves anyway.
The Reeve's Seal is required before continuing.
[icons: idiot, peat_bog]

--- Card 905 · Step IV.E.3.3(d) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE GRIEVANCES
Recalculate Convoy Capacity using the results from Step III.A.4.6, not the results from Step I.C.3.1, unless Dana objects within five seconds.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 906 · Step IV.E.3.4(f) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SALTING WITH BRINE
The player holding the most Mutton becomes the Warden of Mutton for this Segment. The Warden must move the Unwelcome Bard one hex toward the nearest Barley Rise before anyone else may continue.
[icons: mutton, bard, barley_rise]

--- Card 907 · Step IV.E.4.1(c) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SALTING WITH BRINE
Any player may declare an objection to Step III.D.4.4. Objections are resolved by comparing Spoilage Value; the loser must stand for the remainder of the Construction Inspection Phase.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 908 · CLARIFICATION · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
CLARIFICATION
"Adjacent" means sharing an edge. "Near" means sharing an edge or an opinion.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 909 · Step IV.E.4.2(d) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILED GOODS DISPOSAL
The player holding the most Gossip becomes the Warden of Mutton for this Segment. The Warden must pay one Mutton to the Tax Assessor before anyone else may continue.
The Reeve's Seal is required before continuing.
[icons: gossip, mutton]

--- Card 910 · Step IV.E.4.2(d) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILED GOODS DISPOSAL
A player may appeal the result during the Levy Stage. Appeals are resolved using this same Step.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 911 · Step IV.E.4.3(d) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SALTING WITH BRINE
Recalculate Supply Level using the results from Step III.C.6.9, not the results from Step I.D.6.9, unless all players agree, which they will not.
Record the result in the Ledger.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 912 · Step IV.E.4.3(d) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SALTING WITH BRINE
If it is the first round of the Lean Weeks, skip this Step. Players who skip it must still complete Step III.B.4.3, which depends on it.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 913 · Step IV.E.4.3(d): Example · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SALTING WITH BRINE
Mike controls 2 Wattle Fences, and their Hedge Pressure is 3. Following Step IV.A.1.7, Dana must hide a Blight cube on their Household screen.
Everyone agrees this is fair. (It is not.)
[icons: fence, blight]

--- Card 914 · Step IV.E.4.4(a) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
RECORDING THE RESULT
Recalculate Fatigue Points using the results from Step III.C.3.2, not the results from Step II.C.2.8, unless a Tithe Barn is within two hexes.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 915 · Step IV.E.5.1(d) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE ROLL
Gerald, the Communal Ox inspects each Drover in turn. For each one, the app rolls the Harvest Die: on a 13, Dana must stand for the remainder of the Material Allocation Phase; on any other result, recalculate Axle Wear.
The Reeve's Seal is required before continuing.
[icons: ox, drover]

--- Card 916 · Step IV.E.5.1(d) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE ROLL
Players who finished this Step first must wait for the others while looking patient.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 917 · Step IV.E.5.2(a) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE ROLL
Each player whose Axle Wear exceeds 3 must recount their Loam aloud, slowly. Players whose Axle Wear equals exactly 5 must do so twice, for reasons explained in Step III.D.4.4.
Do not continue until every player's Axle Wear is recorded.
[icons: loam]

--- Card 918 · Step IV.E.5.2(a) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE ROLL
Any Blight cube gained during this Step is held in escrow until Step II.B.5.5, then released, then taken back.
[icons: blight]

--- Card 919 · Step IV.E.5.2(a): Example · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE ROLL
Mike controls 6 Cart Roads, and their Yield Modifier is 5. Following Step II.C.2.2, Rosa must stand for the remainder of the Initiative Determination Phase.
Everyone agrees this is fair.
[icons: cart_road]

--- Card 920 · Step IV.E.5.3(b) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SALTING WITH BRINE
The Tinker inspects each Cart Road in turn. For each one, the app rolls the Harvest Die: on a 13, Dana must offer Mike a trade they are obligated to refuse; on any other result, recalculate Morale Modifier.
[icons: tinker, cart_road]

--- Card 921 · Step IV.E.5.3(b) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SALTING WITH BRINE
Any player who sighed during this Step adds 1 Grudge token to their Household.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 922 · Step IV.E.5.4(f) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILED GOODS DISPOSAL
Gerald, the Communal Ox inspects each Cottage in turn. For each one, the app rolls the Harvest Die: on a 13, Rosa must place a Ledger page on the Wicker Marsh; on any other result, recalculate Hedge Pressure.
[icons: ox, cottage, ledger, wicker_marsh]

--- Card 923 · Step IV.E.6.1(b) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILED GOODS DISPOSAL
Each player calculates their Axle Wear: add the number of Wattle Fences they control, subtract the number of Blight cubes they hold, then halve the result (round toward the Reeve).
[icons: fence, blight]

--- Card 924 · Step IV.E.6.2(f) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE ROLL
The active player taps each Scarecrow they control, in clockwise order. For each one next to the Barley Rise, add 1 to their Supply Level.
[icons: scarecrow, barley_rise]

--- Card 925 · Step IV.E.6.2(f) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
SPOILAGE ROLL
If the Goose moved during this Step, undo the Step, then undo the undoing.
[icons: mutton, turnip, blight]

--- Card 926 · CLARIFICATION · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
CLARIFICATION
Turnip and Mutton are different resources, though they taste similar.
[icons: turnip, mutton]


======================================================================
TURN 1 · STAGE V: THE LEVY STAGE
======================================================================

--- Card 927 · Stage V · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
THE LEVY STAGE
The Tax Assessor collects what is owed, and players dispute it.
This Stage has 4 Phases: Tax Assessor Movement, Assessment, Appeals, and Appeals of Appeals.
[icons: assessor, cart_road, ledger]


======================================================================
TURN 1 · STAGE V · PHASE A: TAX ASSESSOR MOVEMENT
======================================================================

--- Card 928 · Stage V · Phase A · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
TAX ASSESSOR MOVEMENT
Before beginning this Phase, confirm that the previous Phase has ended. If it has not, it ends now.
[icons: assessor, cart_road]

--- Card 929 · Step V.A.1.1(f) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR LUNCH BREAK
The Goose inspects each Cart Road in turn. For each one, the app rolls the Harvest Die: on a 13, Mike must hide a Resource card on their Household screen; on any other result, recalculate Morale Modifier.
[icons: goose, cart_road, card_back]

--- Card 930 · Step V.A.1.1(f) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR LUNCH BREAK
Should the Village Idiot disagree with the result, the Village Idiot's result is used.
[icons: idiot]

--- Card 931 · Step V.A.1.2(a) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR ROUTE CALCULATION
The Unwelcome Bard inspects each Cart Road in turn. For each one, the app rolls the Harvest Die: on a 13, Rosa must record the transaction on a Ledger page; on any other result, recalculate Stew Points.
[icons: bard, cart_road]

--- Card 932 · Step V.A.1.3(a) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR LUNCH BREAK
Any player may declare an objection to Step I.D.5.3. Objections are resolved by comparing Axle Wear; the loser must remove a Scarecrow from the board and apologize to it.
[icons: scarecrow]

--- Card 933 · Step V.A.1.4(a) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR ROUTE CALCULATION
The app lists every Peat gained since Step III.A.2.8. Each player confirms their total by tapping it. Totals that are not confirmed within the Dawdling Hourglass are forfeited.
Do not continue until every player's Axle Wear is recorded.
[icons: peat]

--- Card 934 · CLARIFICATION · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
CLARIFICATION
"Adjacent" means sharing an edge, not a corner.
[icons: assessor, cart_road]

--- Card 935 · Step V.A.1.5(c) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR ROUTE CALCULATION
The Goose inspects each Cart Road in turn. For each one, the app rolls the Harvest Die: on a 13, Dana must gain 2 Turnip, then lose 3 Peat; on any other result, recalculate Fatigue Points.
Do not continue until every player's Fatigue Points is recorded.
[icons: goose, cart_road, turnip, peat]

--- Card 936 · Step V.A.1.6(a) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR LUNCH BREAK
Before continuing, every player confirms on screen that Step II.B.2.1 was completed. If anyone is unsure, return to Step III.A.6.4.
[icons: assessor, cart_road]

--- Card 937 · Step V.A.2.1(f) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
RESOLVING DISCREPANCIES
The Tax Assessor inspects each Cart Road in turn. For each one, the app rolls the Harvest Die: on a 13, Rosa must remove a Cart Road from the board and apologize to it; on any other result, recalculate Hedge Pressure.
Record the result in the Ledger.
[icons: assessor, cart_road]

--- Card 938 · Step V.A.2.2(c) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR DETOURS
Any player may declare an objection to Step IV.E.5.8. Objections are resolved by comparing Hedge Pressure; the loser must recount their Turnip aloud, slowly.
Record the result in the Ledger.
[icons: turnip]

--- Card 939 · Step V.A.2.2(c) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR DETOURS
If two players are tied, the player with the lower Stew Points breaks the tie, unless the Reeve is facing north.
[icons: reeve]

--- Card 940 · Step V.A.2.3(b) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR ROUTE CALCULATION
Compare each player's Wicker to their Flax. The difference is that player's Grudge Weight for the rest of this Phase. It will be needed in Step V.A.4.5.
[icons: wicker, flax]

--- Card 941 · Step V.A.2.4(b) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
RECORDING THE RESULT
The player holding the most Gossip becomes the Warden of Flax for this Segment. The Warden must skip the Brine Ration Phase before anyone else may continue.
[icons: gossip, flax]

--- Card 942 · Step V.A.2.4(b) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
RECORDING THE RESULT
If the Goose moved during this Step, undo the Step, then undo the undoing.
[icons: assessor, cart_road]

--- Card 943 · Step V.A.2.5(a) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
RETROACTIVE ADJUSTMENT

--- Card 944 · Step V.A.2.5(a) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
RETROACTIVE ADJUSTMENT

[icons: grudge]

--- Card 945 · Step V.A.3.1(c) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR ROUTE CALCULATION
The active player may spend 1 Gossip to reduce their Initiative Rating by 5. This may be done no more than twice per Segment, and never during the Harvest Die Roll Phase.
[icons: gossip]

--- Card 946 · Step V.A.3.2(d) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---

--- Card 947 · Step V.A.3.3(f) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ORDER OF RESOLUTION
The app rolls the Harvest Die. On a 5 or higher, proceed to the next Step. Otherwise, the active player must discard one Tallow for each Cart Road they control, then repeat this Step.
Do not continue until every player's Grudge Weight is recorded.
[icons: tallow, cart_road]

--- Card 948 · Step V.A.3.4(b) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR DETOURS

[icons: assessor, cart_road, mutton_downs]

--- Card 949 · Step V.A.3.4(b) (cont.) · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
ASSESSOR DETOURS
The Morale Modifier calculated here is used three more times this Turn. Players are advised to remember it.
[icons: assessor, cart_road]
